Mathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier

IsFreeGroupoidclassMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier, predicate
Wording
G is a free groupoid (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then we can say that $G$ is a free groupoid.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{IsFreeGroupoid} (G)\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors nous pouvons dire que $G$ est un groupoïde libre.
IsFreeGroupoid.GeneratorsdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier, type
Wording
generator of G (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Then a generator of $G$ is an element of the function that maps $G$ to $G$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{Generators} (G):= \lambda G . G$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Alors un générateur de $G$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $G$ à $G$.
IsFreeGroupoid.SpanningTree.functorOfMonoidHomdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier, function or constant
Wording
the functor of T and f (from the name)
English
Let $T$ be a wide subquiver of $\operatorname{Symmetrify}$ applied to the set of generators of $G$. Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of the set of ends of $\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'}$ applied to $T$ and $X$. Then the functor of $T$ and $f$ is a functor of $G$ and the set of single objects of $X$.
Formula
$T \in \mathrm{WideSubquiver} (\operatorname{Symmetrify} (\operatorname{Generators} (G))), f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (\operatorname{End} (\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'} (T)), X)\vdash \operatorname{functorOfMonoidHom} (T , f)\in \operatorname{Functor} (G , \operatorname{SingleObj} (X))$
French
Soit $T$ un sous-carquois wide de $\operatorname{Symmetrify}$ appliqué à l'ensemble de générateurs de $G$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de l'ensemble de end de $\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'}$ appliqué à $T$ et $X$. Alors le foncteur de $T$ et $f$ est un foncteur de $G$ et l'ensemble d'objets single de $X$.

IsFreeGroupoid.SpanningTree.loopOfHomdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier, function or constant
Wording
the loop of T and p (from the name)
English
Let $T$ be a wide subquiver of $\operatorname{Symmetrify}$ applied to the set of generators of $G$. Let $p$ be a homomorphism of $a$ and $b$. Then the loop of $T$ and $p$ is an end of $\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'}$ applied to $T$ defined as $\operatorname{of}$ applied to the composition of the tree homomorphism of $T$ and $a$ and the composition of $p$ and the inverse of the tree homomorphism of $T$ and $b$.
Formula
$T \in \mathrm{WideSubquiver} (\operatorname{Symmetrify} (\operatorname{Generators} (G))), p \in \operatorname{Hom} (a , b)\vdash \operatorname{loopOfHom} (T , p):= \operatorname{of} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{treeHom} (T , a), \operatorname{comp} (p , \operatorname{inv} (\operatorname{treeHom} (T , b)))))\in \operatorname{End} (\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'} (T))$
French
Soit $T$ un sous-carquois wide de $\operatorname{Symmetrify}$ appliqué à l'ensemble de générateurs de $G$. Soit $p$ un homomorphisme de $a$ et $b$. Alors la boucle de $T$ et $p$ est un end de $\mathrm{\_private\_\_Mathlib\_\_GroupTheory\_\_FreeGroup\_\_NielsenSchreier\_\_0\_\_IsFreeGroupoid\_\_SpanningTree\_\_root'}$ appliqué à $T$ défini comme $\operatorname{of}$ appliqué à la composition de l'homomorphisme d'arbres de $T$ et $a$ et la composition de $p$ et l'inverse de l'homomorphisme d'arbres de $T$ et $b$.

IsFreeGroupoid.ofdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.NielsenSchreier, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{1}$ be a homomorphism of $a$ and $b$. Then $\operatorname{of}$ applied to $x_{1}$ is a homomorphism of $a$ and $b$ defined as $\operatorname{proj1}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$x_{1} \in \operatorname{Hom} (a , b)\vdash \operatorname{of} (x_{1}):= \operatorname{proj1} (\mathrm{self} , x_{1})\in \operatorname{Hom} (a , b)$
French
Soit $x_{1}$ un homomorphisme de $a$ et $b$. Alors $\operatorname{of}$ appliqué à $x_{1}$ est un homomorphisme de $a$ et $b$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj1}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $x_{1}$.

FreeGroup.startsWithdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.Orbit, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $w$ be an element of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x$ and $\mathrm{Bool}$. Then $\operatorname{startsWith}$ applied to $w$ is an element of the power set of the set of free groups of $x$ defined as $\operatorname{ofPred}$ applied to the function that maps $g$ to the proposition that $\mathrm{GetElem?\_\_getElem?}$ applied to the word of $g$ and $0$ is equal to $\operatorname{some}$ applied to $w$.
Formula
$w \in \mathrm{Prod} (x , \mathrm{Bool})\vdash \operatorname{startsWith} (w):= \operatorname{ofPred} (\lambda g . \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{GetElem?\_\_getElem?} (\operatorname{toWord} (g), 0)}{\operatorname{some} (w)})\in \wp \mathrm{FreeGroup} (x)$
French
Soit $w$ un élément de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $x$ et $\mathrm{Bool}$. Alors $\operatorname{startsWith}$ appliqué à $w$ est un élément de la puissance de l'ensemble de groupes libres de $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{ofPred}$ appliqué à la fonction qui envoie $g$ à la proposition que $\mathrm{GetElem?\_\_getElem?}$ appliqué à le mot de $g$ et $0$ est égal à $\operatorname{some}$ appliqué à $w$.

FreeAddGroup.normdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.Reduce, function or constant
Wording
the norm of x2 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a free additive group of $x$. Then the norm of $x_{2}$ is a natural number defined as $\operatorname{length}$ applied to the word of $x_{2}$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \mathrm{FreeAddGroup} (x)\vdash \operatorname{norm} (x_{2}):= \operatorname{length} (\operatorname{toWord} (x_{2}))\in \Natural$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un groupe additif libre de $x$. Alors la norme de $x_{2}$ est un nombre naturel défini comme $\operatorname{length}$ appliqué à le mot de $x_{2}$.

FreeAddGroup.reduce.churchRosserdefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.Reduce, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Assume that $\operatorname{Red}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Assume that $\operatorname{Red}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $x_{3}$. Then $\operatorname{churchRosser}$ applied to $H_{12}$ and $H_{13}$ is an element of $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{\mathrm{and} (\operatorname{Red} (x_{2} , x_{5}), \operatorname{Red} (x_{3} , x_{5}))}$ defined as the maximal reduction of $x_{1}$.
Formula
$\operatorname{Red} (x_{1} , x_{2}), \operatorname{Red} (x_{1} , x_{3})\vdash \operatorname{churchRosser} (H_{12} , H_{13}):= \operatorname{reduce} (x_{1})\in \SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{\mathrm{and} (\operatorname{Red} (x_{2} , x_{5}), \operatorname{Red} (x_{3} , x_{5}))}$
French

Alors $\operatorname{churchRosser}$ appliqué à $H_{12}$ et $H_{13}$ est un élément de $\SubtypeSet {x_{5}}{\mathrm{and} (\operatorname{Red} (x_{2} , x_{5}), \operatorname{Red} (x_{3} , x_{5}))}$ défini comme le reduction maximal de $x_{1}$.
FreeAddGroup.toWorddefMathlib.GroupTheory.FreeGroup.Reduce, function or constant
Wording
the word of x2 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a free additive group of $x$. Then the word of $x_{2}$ is a list of elements of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x$ and $\mathrm{Bool}$ defined as $\operatorname{lift}$ applied to $\operatorname{reduce}$, the function that maps $x_{2}$, $x_{3}$ and $H$ to $\operatorname{eq}$ applied to $H$ and $x_{2}$.

French

Alors le mot de $x_{2}$ est une liste d'éléments de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $x$ et $\mathrm{Bool}$ défini comme $\operatorname{lift}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{reduce}$, la fonction qui envoie $x_{2}$, $x_{3}$ et $H$ à $\operatorname{eq}$ appliqué à $H$ et $x_{2}$.

Wording

English

Group.Generators.liftdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Generators, function or constant
Wording
the lift of P (from the name)
English
Let $P$ be a generator of $G$ and $x$. Then the lift of $P$ is a monoid homomorphism of the set of free groups of $x$ and $G$ defined as $\operatorname{lift}$ applied to $\operatorname{val}$ applied to $P$.
Formula
$P \in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x)\vdash \operatorname{lift} (P):= \operatorname{lift} (\operatorname{val} (P))\in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (\mathrm{FreeGroup} (x), G)$
French
Soit $P$ un générateur de $G$ et $x$. Alors le lift de $P$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes de l'ensemble de groupes libres de $x$ et $G$ défini comme $\operatorname{lift}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{val}$ appliqué à $P$.
Group.Generators.mapdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Generators, function or constant
Wording
the map of P and f (from the name)
English
Let $P$ be a generator of $G$ and $x$. Let $f$ be a monoid homomorphism of $G$ and $H$ such that $\operatorname{Surjective}$ holds for $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the map of $P$ and $f$ is a generator of $H$ and $x$.
Formula
$P \in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x), f \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (G , H), \operatorname{Surjective} (\operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{map} (P , f , \mathrm{hf})\in \operatorname{Generators} (H , x)$
French
Soit $P$ un générateur de $G$ et $x$. Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $G$ et $H$ tel que $\operatorname{Surjective}$ tienne pour $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors l'application de $P$ et $f$ est un générateur de $H$ et $x$.
Group.Generators.ofFreeGroupHomdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Generators, function or constant
Wording
the generator of x2 and h (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a monoid homomorphism of the set of free groups of $x$ and $G$ such that $\operatorname{Surjective}$ holds for $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Then the generator of $x_{2}$ and $h$ is a generator of $G$ and $x$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (\mathrm{FreeGroup} (x), G), \operatorname{Surjective} (\operatorname{coe} (x_{2}))\vdash \operatorname{ofFreeGroupHom} (x_{2} , h)\in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x)$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes de l'ensemble de groupes libres de $x$ et $G$ tel que $\operatorname{Surjective}$ tienne pour $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $x_{2}$. Alors le générateur de $x_{2}$ et $h$ est un générateur de $G$ et $x$.
Group.Generators.ofSetdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Generators, function or constant
Wording
the generator of h (from the name)
English
Assume that the closure of $S$ is equal to the top. Then the generator of $h$ is a generator of $G$ and the set of elements of $S$.
Formula
$\EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{closure} (S)}{\operatorname{top}}\vdash \operatorname{ofSet} (h)\in \operatorname{Generators} (G , \operatorname{Elem} (S))$
French
Supposons que la clôture de $S$ est égale au top. Alors le générateur de $h$ est un générateur de $G$ et l'ensemble d'éléments de $S$.

Wording

English
Let $P$ be a generator of $G$ and $x$. Assume that $\operatorname{Surjective}$ holds for $e$. Then the reindexing of $P$ and $\mathrm{he}$ is a generator of $G$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$P \in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x), \operatorname{Surjective} (e)\vdash \operatorname{reindex} (P , \mathrm{he})\in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x_{1})$

Group.Generators.valdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Generators, function or constant
Wording
the value of self and x2 (from the name)
English
Let $\mathrm{self}$ be a generator of $G$ and $x_{1}$. Let $x_{2}$ be an element of $x_{1}$. Then the value of $\mathrm{self}$ and $x_{2}$ is an element of $G$ defined as $\operatorname{proj0}$ applied to $\mathrm{self}$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{self} \in \operatorname{Generators} (G , x_{1}), x_{2} \in x_{1} \vdash \operatorname{val} (\mathrm{self} , x_{2}):= \operatorname{proj0} (\mathrm{self} , x_{2})\in G$
French
Soit $\mathrm{self}$ un générateur de $G$ et $x_{1}$. Soit $x_{2}$ un élément de $x_{1}$. Alors la valeur de $\mathrm{self}$ et $x_{2}$ est un élément de $G$ défini comme $\operatorname{proj0}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{self}$ et $x_{2}$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.Goursat

AddSubgroup.goursatFstdefMathlib.GroupTheory.Goursat, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $I$ be an additive subgroup of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $G$ and $H$. Then $\operatorname{goursatFst}$ applied to $I$ is an additive subgroup of $G$ defined as the map of the composition of the first component of $G$ and $H$ and the subtype of $I$ and the additive kernel of the composition of the second component of $G$ and $H$ and the subtype of $I$.
Formula
$I \in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (\mathrm{Prod} (G , H))\vdash \operatorname{goursatFst} (I):= \operatorname{map} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{fst} (G , H), \operatorname{subtype} (I)), \operatorname{ker} (\operatorname{comp} (\operatorname{snd} (G , H), \operatorname{subtype} (I))))\in \mathrm{AddSubgroup} (G)$
French
Soit $I$ un sous-groupe additif de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $G$ et $H$. AddAction.IsBlockdefMathlib.GroupTheory.GroupAction.Blocks, predicate
Wording
B is a block (from the name)
English
Let $G$ be a set. Let $B$ be an element of $\wp X$. Then $B$ is a block, if for all elements $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ of $G$, if the sum of $x_{1}$ and $B$ is distinct from the sum of $x_{2}$ and $B$, then the sum of $x_{1}$ and $B$ is disjoint to the sum of $x_{2}$ and $B$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} , B \in \wp X \vdash \operatorname{IsBlock} (G , B):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{1} \in G . \forall x_{2} \in G . \operatorname{hVAdd} (x_{1} , B)\neq \operatorname{hVAdd} (x_{2} , B)\rightarrow \mathrm{Disjoint} (\operatorname{hVAdd} (x_{1} , B), \operatorname{hVAdd} (x_{2} , B))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Soit $B$ un élément de $\wp X$. Alors $B$ est un bloc si pour tous les éléments $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ de $G$, si la somme de $x_{1}$ et $B$ est distincte de la somme de $x_{2}$ et $B$, alors la somme de $x_{1}$ et $B$ est disjointe à la somme de $x_{2}$ et $B$.

Let $g$ be a function from elements of $x_{1}$ to elements of $X$ such that $\operatorname{LeftInverse}$ holds for $g$ and $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$ and $\operatorname{RightInverse}$ holds for $g$ and $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the inverse of $f$ and $g$ is an additive action homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $X$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{AddActionHom} (\mathrm{id} , X , x_{1}), g \in x_{1} \rightarrow X , \operatorname{LeftInverse} (g , \operatorname{coe} (f)), \operatorname{RightInverse} (g , \operatorname{coe} (f))\vdash \operatorname{inverse} (f , g , h , h_{1})\in \mathrm{AddActionHom} (\mathrm{id} , x_{1} , X)$
French

AddAction.SupportsdefMathlib.GroupTheory.GroupAction.Support, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $G$ be a set. Let $s$ be an element of $\wp x$. Let $b$ be an element of $x_{1}$. Then $\operatorname{Supports}$ holds for $G$, $s$ and $b$, if for all elements $g$ of $G$, if for all elements $a$ of $x$, if $a$ is an element of $s$, then the sum of $g$ and $a$ is equal to $a$, then the sum of $g$ and $b$ is equal to $b$.

the equivalence of f (from the name)

Mathlib.GroupTheory.HNNExtension

HNNExtensiondefMathlib.GroupTheory.HNNExtension, type
Wording
no wording
English
Let $G$ be a set. Let $A$ and $B$ be subgroups of $G$. Let $x_{1}$ be a multiplicative equivalence of $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {A}{x_{2}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {B}{x_{3}})}$. Then an element of $\mathrm{HNNExtension}$ applied to $G$, $A$, $B$ and $x_{1}$ is an element of the function that maps $G$, $A$, $B$ and $x_{1}$ to the set of quotients of the congruence relation of $B$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$G \in \mathrm{Set} , A , B \in \mathrm{Subgroup} (G), x_{1} \in \mathrm{MulEquiv} (\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {A}{x_{2}})}, \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {B}{x_{3}})})\vdash \mathrm{HNNExtension} (G , A , B , x_{1}):= \lambda G , A , B , x_{1} . \operatorname{Quotient} (\operatorname{con} (G , A , B , x_{1}))$
French
Soit $G$ un ensemble. Soient $A$ et $B$ des sous-groupes de $G$. Soit $x_{1}$ une équivalence multiplicative de $\SubtypeSet {x_{2}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {A}{x_{2}})}$ et $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {B}{x_{3}})}$. Alors un élément de $\mathrm{HNNExtension}$ appliqué à $G$, $A$, $B$ et $x_{1}$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $G$, $A$, $B$ et $x_{1}$ à l'ensemble de quotients de la relation de congruences de $B$ et $x_{1}$.

French

Then $\operatorname{awayToAwayRight}$ applied to $x_{3}$, $F$, $y$ and $G$ is a monoid homomorphism of $N$ and $P$.
Formula
$x_{3} \in M , F \in \operatorname{AwayMap} (x_{3} , N), y \in M , G \in \operatorname{AwayMap} (x_{3} \times y , P)\vdash \operatorname{awayToAwayRight} (x_{3} , F , y , G)\in \mathrm{MonoidHom} (N , P)$
French
Soient $x_{3}$ et $y$ des éléments de $M$. Soit $F$ un élément de $\operatorname{AwayMap}$ appliqué à $x_{3}$ et $N$. Soit $G$ un élément de $\operatorname{AwayMap}$ appliqué à $x_{3} \times y$ et $P$. Alors $\operatorname{awayToAwayRight}$ appliqué à $x_{3}$, $F$, $y$ et $G$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes de $N$ et $P$.

Mathlib.GroupTheory.MonoidLocalization.MonoidWithZero

Submonoid.LocalizationMap.lift₀defMathlib.GroupTheory.MonoidLocalization.MonoidWithZero, function or constant
Wording
the lift of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a localization map of $S$ and $N$. Let $g$ be a monoid with zero homomorphism of $M$ and $P$ such that for all elements $y$ of $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {S}{x_{3}})}$, $g$ applied to $y$ is a unit. Then the lift of $f$ and $g$ is a monoid with zero homomorphism of $N$ and $P$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{LocalizationMap} (S , N), g \in \mathrm{MonoidWithZeroHom} (M , P), \forall y \in \SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoAA {S}{x_{3}})}. \mathrm{IsUnit} (g (y))\vdash \mathrm{Submonoid\_\_LocalizationMap\_\_lift\_u2080\_} (f , g , \mathrm{hg})\in \mathrm{MonoidWithZeroHom} (N , P)$
French

Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de monoïdes avec du zéro de $M$ et $P$ tel que pour tous les éléments $y$ de $\SubtypeSet {x_{3}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoT {S}{x_{3}})}$, $g$ appliqué à $y$ soit une unité. Alors le lift de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme de monoïdes avec du zéro de $N$ et $P$.
